### Short Project Description*  

> **English version直接填：**

LiqPass is a parametric “liquidation insurance” layer for high-leverage perp traders.  
Retail traders pay a small, fixed premium (e.g. on a 100× BTC position) to cap their worst-case loss if liquidation happens within a defined window (8h / 24h / monthly).  

The system only trusts:  
- on-chain premium payment events, and  
- read-only exchange APIs (OKX, Binance, etc.),  

to build a reproducible evidence bundle (normalized JSON → Merkle root → on-chain anchor). Claims become verifiable, audit-friendly, and eventually automatable.  

On Polygon, LiqPass will plug into perp protocols and aggregators to (1) grow their fee volume, and (2) give smaller traders a budgeted downside so they dare to trade with size.

---

### What have you shipped that people can use right now? (links encouraged)  

You can填成一段，非常具体、强调“已经干了啥”：

We have already shipped a working MVP:

- **On-chain checkout contract** (Base mainnet): collects USDC premiums and emits `PremiumPaid` events for each policy.  
- **Backend & verification services**: a TypeScript backend and a Python verification micro-service that read OKX / Binance via read-only API keys, normalize liquidation data, and return a Merkle-rooted evidence package.  
- **User-facing web app**: wallet login, policy purchase flow, order/claims dashboards, and a basic evidence viewer are live at:  
  - App / landing: **https://wjz5788.com/**  
  - Code: **https://github.com/wjz5788/LiqPass**  

Right now, early users can:  

1. Connect a wallet,  
2. Buy a short-term liquidation cover on Base,  
3. Submit an exchange order ID after liquidation,  
4. Get a deterministic verification result and an evidence bundle that can be manually reviewed for payout.

Our next step is to deploy the core flow on **Polygon (PoS or zkEVM)** and integrate with Polygon perp venues.

---

你可以多选，然后在下面的开放问题写一段说明：

> **What kind of support would be most helpful to supercharge your business?**

推荐填法（直接用）：

1. **Grant support for core Polygon deployment**  
   - Port existing Base mainnet MVP (checkout + policy contracts) to Polygon (PoS / zkEVM).  
   - Build the first Polygon-native product SKU: 24h liquidation cover for BTC/USDC on a Polygon perp venue.  
   - Fund engineering time for: contract hardening, backend refactors, and Polygon indexer / event listeners.

2. **GTM and integrations on Polygon**  
   - Co-design pilots with 1–2 Polygon perp protocols or aggregators, where users can buy LiqPass covers directly from the trading UI.  
   - Run a small, time-boxed incentive campaign: subsidized premiums for early Polygon users in exchange for product feedback and metrics.  

3. **Security & robustness**  
   - Support for an external audit of the Polygon smart contracts and the off-chain verification logic (at least a focused review of the evidence → payout pipeline).  

4. **Founder coaching / cohort**  
   - I am a solo builder; joining a Polygon-native cohort would help with strategy, hiring contributors, and prioritizing the most impactful Polygon integrations first.

- **Current status:**

We are **planning a small pre-seed / angel round** once:  

1. The Polygon deployment is live with at least one integration, and  
2. We have real usage metrics (policies sold, premiums, claims, loss ratio) from Polygon users.  

Support from “Grow on Polygon” would help us reach that proof-of-traction stage faster on Polygon itself, rather than raising too early.

### Brief Project Description*  

用一段话把产品、技术路线、Polygon 相关性说清楚（可以直接用这段）：

LiqPass is a parametric “liquidation insurance” layer for high-leverage perp traders.  
Retail traders pay a small, fixed premium on their leveraged BTC/USDC or BTC/USDT positions to cap worst-case losses if liquidation occurs within a defined time window (8h / 24h / monthly).

The system only trusts:  

- On-chain premium payment events, and  
- Read-only exchange APIs (OKX, Binance, etc.),  

to build a normalized evidence bundle (canonical JSON → Merkle root → on-chain anchor). This makes every claim reproducible, auditable, and eventually automatable.

Today, the MVP runs on Base mainnet. The next step is to deploy LiqPass on Polygon, integrate with Polygon-perp venues, and offer “one-click: open position + add liquidation cover” for Polygon users.

---

### What gives your team a right to win / advantage in this space?*  

你是 solo builder，就把“深度 + 执行 + 方向专注”说清楚，不装团队军团：

I am a solo founder who has been obsessed with the “retail degen + risk cap” problem for a long time and have already shipped a full vertical slice: smart contracts, backend verification services, and a working front-end.

A few specific advantages:

- **End-to-end ownership**: I directly build contracts, backend infra, and UI, so iteration from idea → shipped feature → user feedback is fast.  
- **Evidence-first design**: The entire system is designed around verifiable evidence (normalized JSON, Merkle roots, on-chain anchors) instead of black-box off-chain logic. This makes it easier to integrate with exchanges, auditors, and regulators later.  
- **Narrow, deep focus**: I focus on one very clear use case—high-leverage liquidation risk—and do it deeply (risk parameters, payout caps, loss ratios), instead of being a general-purpose DeFi app.  
- **EVM-native thinking**: The architecture is designed to be portable across EVM chains (Base, Polygon, etc.), so adding Polygon is an extension of work already done, not a restart.

With Polygon’s ecosystem and GTM support, this can become the default liquidation-cover layer for EVM perp trading.

---

> 🌐 **引用来源**:
> 1. [Season 8 Intent](https://gov.optimism.io/t/season-8-intent/10009?utm_source=chatgpt.com)
> 2. [Season 8 Intent](https://gov.optimism.io/t/season-8-intent/10009?utm_source=chatgpt.com)

> 4. [Superchain Sunday: Week 36 2025](https://www.superchain.eco/insights/superchain-sunday-week-36-2025?utm_source=chatgpt.com)

这个「增长补助金」就是 Optimism / Superchain Season 8 的 **Growth Grants**，专门给“已经上线、准备拉 TVL & 交易费”的应用发 OP 的那一档。你现在打开的就是它。

我直接帮你把它跟 LiqPass 绑定一下，看怎么写、值不值得冲一把。

---

## 1. 这个增长补助金要什么？

官方重点可以粗暴理解成两句：citeturn0search1turn0search11  

1. **让 Superchain 上的 TVL 长胖**  
   - 尤其是稳定币、桥入资产、可以参与 Interop 的“可互操作 TVL”。  
2. **让 Superchain 上的应用产生更多费用（gas + 协议费）**  
   - 希望看到「因为你的项目存在 → 用户多做了交易 → 链和协议多赚了钱」。

你截的那段中文里说的两项成功指标，其实就是：

- **超级链 TVL 光圈**：可以理解成「在 OP Stack 生态里、符合互操作标准的 TVL 总量」。  
- **超级链交易费用**：应用产生的 gas、协议费、或者 AOP（application-owned protocol fees）。

他们要你做的事：

- 提交一份 **增长计划（Growth Plan）**
- 明确 **KPI / success metrics**
- 说清楚 **OP 资金怎么用来驱动这些指标**

---

## 2. 你现在这个项目算不算“合适的项目”？

从条件对比来看，你是「高度适配」的那一类，只是要在表述上动点手脚：

**你的现状：**

- LiqPass 合约已经在 **Base 主网** 部署，是一个真实 onchain 应用（参数化保险 / 爆仓保）。
- 场景直接绑定：高杠杆 perp 交易（BTC/USDC、BTC/USDT），天然是 **TVL + 交易费放大器**。
- 路线是：  
  “保险 → 降低爆仓焦虑 → 更多人敢上高杠杆 / 加大仓位 / 延长持仓 → perp 协议 TVL & fee 上升”。

**和 Growth Grants 要求的对齐点：**

- *“提升 Superchain TVL、推动跨链交易、创造更强激励机制的工具”*  
  - 你是：**给 Superchain 上的 perp / 衍生品协议叠一层“损失上限 / 回撤保底”的 risk wrapper**。  
  - 可以描述为：**risk infra / safety layer**，帮助把 CEX 杠杆用户搬到 OP Stack 链上。
- 你可以承诺的指标：
  - 由「购买 LiqPass 的用户」贡献的 **额外保证金 / open interest**（TVL）
  - 这些用户在合作协议上产生的 **交易费 / gas**（fees）

**链的资格问题：**

- Season 8 全部围绕 **Superchain（OP Stack 链集合）** 展开，只要是标准 OP Stack 链并接入治理 / 安全委员会，理论上都可以享受 Grants。citeturn0search1  
- Base 属于 Superchain 里最主流的一条，**大概率在可支持列表里**。  
- 你页面上那个「请在此处确认您的连锁店是否符合资格 / 查看您的资格」就是一个检测工具——  
  - 如果选 Base 显示合格，直接以 **“LiqPass @ Base”** 申请；  
  - 如果不在当前批次支持范围，可以规划一条 **在 OP Mainnet 部署轻量入口合约** 的路线（但这一步可以放在后面再做）。

---

## 3. 帮你搭一份「增长计划 + KPI」骨架（可直接往申请表里填）

下面这套，你可以当成**第一版草稿**，根据字数限制再缩。

### 3.1 增长目标（对着他们的两项成功指标说）

> **目标 1：为 Superchain 带来“有保险保护”的新增保证金 TVL**  
> - 通过 LiqPass 在 Base / OP 生态里和 1–2 个 perp 协议集成，面向高杠杆用户（≥20x），降低爆仓焦虑。  
> - 目标：在计划期内，**带动 1.5M–3M 美金等值的新增保证金 TVL** 进入 OP Stack 链上的 perp 协议（以有保单用户的保证金余额为口径）。

> **目标 2：提升 Superchain 应用产生的交易费用**  
> - 通过为高频、高杠杆交易者提供「爆仓保 / 回撤保」产品，增加他们的下单频率和持仓时间。  
> - 目标：在计划期内，**通过已投保用户贡献的交易量，带动 3–5 倍于 OP 价值的新增协议费和 gas 费用**（按官方 $/OP TVL / fee 多倍目标对齐）。citeturn3search0turn0search4  

你也可以明确写一句：

> 我们会按 Grants Council 建议，力争实现 **≥ 20–25 美元 TVL / OP** 的资本效率，并在结束时提供完整的 onchain 数据和 Attribution 分析。

### 3.2 执行路径（Growth Plan）

可以写成三条主线：

1. **和 1–2 个 Superchain perp 协议做原生集成**
   - 例：Base / OP Mainnet 上的 perp 协议（留空名字，或者写“我们已在与部分协议初步对接”）。  
   - 在协议前端中嵌入「一键投保」入口：用户开仓时直接看到「24h 爆仓保 / 月度回撤保」报价。  
   - 协议端提供「投保订单」标签，方便跟踪 insured volume / TVL。

2. **面向高杠杆用户的定向增长活动**
   - 使用 OP 作为：
     - **投保返现**（下第一笔保单返一定比例 OP）  
     - **交易里程碑奖励**（在有保单情况下达到某个累计成交额，发 OP 奖励）  
   - 聚焦：从 CEX 高杠杆用户中引流，用「有爆仓保的高杠杆体验」作为差异点，把他们搬到链上。

3. **互操作 / 跨链路径**
   - 先从 Base / OP Mainnet 做起，中期计划将保险逻辑标准化为 **可复用的 risk module**，支持更多 OP Stack 链上的 perp / margin 协议。  
   - 通过统一的保单合约 + 跨链消息（后期），让 TVL 在 Superchain 内迁移时依然能「带着保险走」。

---

### 3.3 里程碑 & KPI 示例（你可以对着他们表格直接填）

假设申请期为 6 个月，里程碑可以这样切：

**里程碑 1：完成与首个 OP Stack perp 协议的集成（第 1–2 个月）**

- 集成内容：
  - 保单购买合约已在 Base / OP Mainnet 主网部署，并完成验证；
  - 合作协议前端完成「一键投保」UI；
  - API / 后端接入，能自动记录有保单用户的 TVL & 成交额。
- KPI：
  - 至少 100 个真实用户完成钱包登录 + 首次投保；
  - 合约产生 ≥ 500 笔保费支付交易。

**里程碑 2：启动激励活动 & 放量试运行（第 3–4 个月）**

- 活动设计：
  - 「首投返现」「带保单交易里程碑奖励」上线；
  - 与合作协议在社媒、社区 AMA 推广。
- KPI：
  - 累计售出 ≥ 1,000 份保单；
  - 由有保单用户贡献的保证金 TVL ≥ 1M USDC 等值；
  - 由有保单用户贡献的协议费用（交易费 + gas） ≥ 50k 美元等值（可以根据实际调整）。

**里程碑 3：扩展到第二个协议 / 链 & 复盘（第 5–6 个月）**

- 扩展：
  - 将 LiqPass 模块复制到第二个 OP Stack 协议 / 链；
  - 形成统一的「保险 + TVL + fee」归因分析报表。
- KPI：
  - 覆盖的协议数 ≥ 2；  
  - Interop 友好的“可保 TVL” ≥ 2M–3M 美元等值；  
  - 形成一份公开的增长报告（TVL / fee / 用户画像 / 风险表现），提交给 Grants Council。

---

## 4. 资金（OP）分配示例（给他们一个“合规 + 高性价比”的结构）

Growth Grants 更喜欢 OP 被用在 **用户增长 / 生态激励**，而不是纯发工资。你可以这样设计一个结构（具体申请多少 OP 再看你心态，但结构可以照抄）：

> **示例：申请 80,000–120,000 OP，分配方案：**
> - **70% 用户激励 & 流动性激励**
>   - 投保返现、交易里程碑奖励、与合作协议共建活动的奖励池。  
>   - 对所有激励设置合理的锁定 / 线性释放以避免纯挖矿行为。
> - **20% 风险准备金 / 协议自有资金（Protocol-owned Liquidity）**
>   - 用作 LiqPass 风险池的一部分，以提高赔付能力、增强用户信任。  
>   - 风险池管理逻辑和资产地址全部 onchain 透明。
> - **10% 执行成本**
>   - 用于集成 bounty、审计费用补贴、监控 & 报表工具等，尽量不直接作为团队工资。

表述时可以强调：

> 团队的日常开发成本主要通过其他 Grant / 投资渠道覆盖，本次增长补助金以 **驱动 Superchain 指标为第一目标**，所有 OP 使用都会提供 onchain 追踪和公开报告。

---

## 5. 实际操作步骤

结合你截图里的「工作原理」两步，可以这样执行：

1. **在 Growth Grants 卡片里点「访问网站」**
   - 选你当前主要部署的链（优先 Base；如果工具支持 Base，直接选）。
   - 在申请表里：
     - 项目名：LiqPass（爆仓保）  
     - 项目类型：risk infra / insurance layer for high-leverage traders on OP Stack  
     - 链接：合约地址、前端 demo、GitHub、wjz5788.com 等。
2. **把上面的 Growth Plan / KPI / 预算结构填进去**
   - 尤其在“如何衡量成功”和“OP 分配方案”那两块，把 TVL / fee 指标写清楚。
3. **提交后留意邮箱 / 治理论坛**
   - 官方说审核平均 15 天左右，这是正常周期。citeturn0search11  

---

总结一下：  
这个增长补助金非常适合把你现在「只能 demo 的 LiqPass」升级成 **“真和两个 OP Stack 协议打通、真带 TVL 和费用的数据点”**。Arbitrum 那边是一个增长引擎，这个是第二个；两边的材料完全可以共用一套“你在做什么 / 指标 / 预算”的内核，只是换一层「链 + 生态叙事的皮」。  

下一步，你可以把他们申请表里具体问题复制一段出来，我直接按这个框架帮你填成可提交版本。

> LiqPass为高杠杆永续合约交易者提供短期清算与降幅保护，支持8小时、24小时及每月覆盖。这将提升风险预算，降低尾部损失的担忧，进而增加超级链永续协议的保证金和交易频率。我们定下现实的目标：80,000 OP激励，吸引1,000至2,000名高杠杆交易者，并估算产生50至200K美元的费用。

>
> **[关于可持续性与收入模式]**

>
> **[关于LiqPass的进展与里程碑]**

---

**1. Project Name**

> LiqPass – Parametric Liquidation Protection for High-Leverage Traders

---

(如果你更想用别的号，就换成自己的链接。)

---

（这里换成你常用、方便接收通知的邮箱。）

---

**4. Demo link**

> https://wjz5788.com/  
> (Current LiqPass MVP running on Base, with liquidation-cover flows wired to read-only exchange APIs.)

---

**5. Is your project fully open source?***

> Partially open source

**5a. If "Partially Open Source", please explain.**

> The core smart contracts and front-end are open source (MIT-style) so that anyone can verify the coverage logic, premium payment flows, and claim events.  
> The off-chain verification and risk engine that touches user exchange API keys (OKX / Binance read-only data) is currently closed-source to reduce attack surface, prevent abuse, and protect fraud-detection heuristics.  
> However, we publish the canonical JSON formats, hashing scheme, Merkle-root commitments, and onchain anchors so that all verification results remain reproducible and independently auditable.

---

**6. List all chains your project will support\***

> - Base – first production deployment for LiqPass liquidation and drawdown cover.  
> - OP Mainnet – deployment during the grant period to support Superchain-native perp protocols.  
> - Additional OP Stack / Superchain chains from the Superchain Index as we integrate with more perp / margin protocols, using the same insurance contracts and verification engine.

---

**7. Total OP Requested**

> 80,000 OP  
>  
> 100% of the OP will be distributed directly to end users and builders who interact with LiqPass (policy buyers, insured traders who reach volume milestones, integrators who ship and maintain Superchain integrations). No OP will be sold or swapped by the team.

---

**8. How much Superchain TVL do you have today?***

> 0  
>  
> LiqPass contracts are currently deployed and live on Base, but we have not yet run a public campaign or measured meaningful insured TVL on eligible Superchain chains. This grant is specifically intended to fund the first scaled rollout that drives trackable Superchain TVL through insured margin on perp protocols.

---

**9. Describe the project and how it will drive TVL or Transaction Fees\***

> LiqPass is a parametric insurance layer for high-leverage perpetual traders on Superchain chains.  
>  
> Traders pay a small fixed premium to cap their downside over a defined window (e.g. 8h / 24h liquidation cover or monthly drawdown protection). When a covered position is liquidated or crosses a drawdown threshold, LiqPass uses read-only exchange APIs (OKX / Binance) plus onchain records to verify the event deterministically and pays out up to a capped percentage of the user’s margin.  
>  
> By capping tail risk, LiqPass gives risk-averse or undercapitalized traders the confidence to:  
> - bridge more capital from CEXs / other chains to Superchain perp protocols,  
> - maintain higher average margin balances on these protocols, and  
> - trade more frequently and with higher notional size.  
>  
> We integrate directly into perp front-ends (e.g. a one-click “Add 24h liquidation cover” option at order placement), and tag insured users, so that incremental TVL and fees can be attributed to LiqPass campaigns.  
>  
> For TVL attribution, we will only count:  
> - net inflows of margin assets (USDC / USDT / ETH) that end up locked in perp margin vaults or LP positions on eligible Superchain chains,  
> - from wallets that purchased LiqPass cover during the campaign window,  
> - measured as a time-weighted average (30–90 days),  
> and we will explicitly exclude idle EOAs and double-counting of LP positions.  
>  
> With this grant we aim, over 6–9 months, to:  
> - onboard 1,000–2,000 unique wallets that purchase at least one policy on eligible Superchain chains,  
> - reach US$1.5–3.0M in average insured margin TVL across supported Superchain perp protocols at steady state, and  
> - generate US$50k–150k in incremental protocol trading fees and gas, based on conservative assumptions about volume and fee rates for high-leverage traders.  
>  
> All policies, payouts, and OP distributions will be recorded in an auditable way (public dashboards, tagged addresses) so that stakeholders can clearly see how LiqPass campaigns contributed to Superchain TVL and transaction fees.

---

**10. Have you received an OP grant from the Gov Fund or Foundation (excluding Retro Funding)? If yes, are these grants fully distributed? What was the outcome of the grant?\***

> No.  
>  
> LiqPass has not previously received any OP grant from the Governance Fund or the Optimism Foundation, and there is no overlap with any Retro Funding round.

---

**11. Milestones to unlock OP distribution\***  
*(first 40% of the OP)*

> **Milestone 1 – First Superchain integrations and 500k insured TVL (unlocks 40% of OP)**  
>  
> We will:  
> - Deploy and verify LiqPass insurance contracts on Base and/or OP Mainnet;  
> - Integrate LiqPass into at least one Superchain perp protocol (front-end integration + tracking tags for insured users);  
> - Launch an initial “insured trading sprint” using up to 32,000 OP (40% of the grant) to:  
>   - refund a portion of premiums in OP to early users who bridge fresh capital and buy cover, and  
>   - reward insured traders who reach minimum trading-volume thresholds on supported perp pairs.  
>  
> **Unlock criteria for Milestone 1:**  
> - ≥ 500 unique addresses on eligible Superchain chains purchase at least one LiqPass policy;  
> - ≥ US$500k in net new insured margin TVL on Superchain perp protocols (30-day trailing average versus baseline at program start), measured without double-counting;  
> - ≥ 60% of the first 40% OP tranche actually distributed to end users / integrators based on onchain actions (policy purchase, trading milestones, integration bounties), with a public dashboard showing distributions;  
> - Public documentation and dashboard live, describing how insured TVL and fees are measured.

---

**12. Final Milestone for Grant Completion\***  
*(last 60% of the OP)*

> **Milestone 2 – Scale-up to 2–3M insured TVL and multiple Superchain protocols (unlocks final 60% of OP)**  
>  
> We will:  
> - Extend LiqPass integration to at least one additional Superchain protocol or chain from the Superchain Index;  
> - Iterate pricing and UX based on observed loss ratios and usage from Milestone 1;  
> - Use the remaining 48,000 OP for sustained but tapered user programs: recurring insured-trading campaigns, refer-a-friend for insured traders, and targeted cashback in OP on bridging and policy purchases.  
>  
> **Unlock criteria for Milestone 2:**  
> - ≥ US$1.5–3.0M in net insured Superchain TVL (90-day trailing average), accounting only for margin locked on eligible Superchain protocols by wallets that interacted with LiqPass campaigns, measured without double-counting;  
> - ≥ 1,500–2,000 unique addresses on eligible Superchain chains with at least one active or expired LiqPass policy;  
> - Demonstrated sustainability: over the last 90 days of the program, premium income (excluding OP incentives) covers expected losses with a target loss ratio between 40–70%, and at least one partner protocol commits to continue offering LiqPass integration without direct OP subsidies;  
> - A public post-mortem / growth report published, plus a live dashboard that tracks insured TVL, fees, and OP distribution for at least 12 months after grant completion.

---

**13. Additional information you consider relevant**

> - **Risk and verification model:** LiqPass is fully non-custodial; we only rely on onchain events plus read-only exchange APIs. All evidence bundles are normalized, hashed, and anchored onchain so that any third party can verify that a payout was justified.  
> - **Alignment with Superchain:** Our product is designed specifically to make high-leverage trading on OP Stack chains less intimidating for small retail traders by giving them a clear budgeted downside. This directly supports TVL growth in Superchain perp protocols and increases sustainable fee generation.  
> - **OP usage discipline:** OP will never be sold or used as risk capital. It will only be distributed to users and builders who take onchain actions that are aligned with the proposal (bridging capital, buying cover, trading on Superchain perp protocols, and shipping integrations). All distributions will be onchain, with addresses and criteria clearly documented.  
> - **Complementary funding:** We may apply for ecosystem-specific grants (e.g. Base, Arbitrum) for development costs, but this Growth Grant is reserved exclusively for Superchain TVL/fee growth. Metrics and budgets will be kept separate to avoid double-counting between ecosystems.

---

这个「增长补助金」就是 Optimism 超级链 Season 8 的 **Growth Grants**。我给你拆成跟 LiqPass 直接相关的版本：

---

## 1. 这个增长补助金，到底在干嘛

核心信息压缩版：

- **归属**：Optimism Grants Council，Season 8 里的一个轨道（Growth Grants）。citeturn0search0turn0search2  
- **预算**：本季大概有 **≈6.3M OP** 专门给“增长”类项目。citeturn0search1  
- **硬指标**：你的计划必须至少命中一条：
  - **Superchain TVL（总锁仓）**  
  - **Superchain Transaction Fees（交易费用）**citeturn0search0  
- **典型适配项目**：  
  - 做跨链互操作，拉动 TVL 的  
  - 做能提升 TVL 的升级 / 产品  
  - 做能推动跨链交易的功能  
  - 或者做“跟踪采用、支持资产向互操作格式迁移、激励使用”的工具类项目citeturn0search0turn0search5  
- **范围**：面向所有 OP Stack 链（你看到的那一串：OP Mainnet、Base、World、Zora、Mode 等等）。Base 在当前超级链里是交易量、TVL、sequencer 收入的绝对大头，所以在 Base 上拉动交易/TVL，是他们高度关注的方向。citeturn0search4turn0search6  

**时间点提醒：**

- 有文章写 Season 8 Grants 当前一轮的申请截止是 **2025-11-12** 左右，你截图里的 `2025-12-04` 很可能是翻译页或 UI 上的显示/更新日期，不是最终截止日。以 **opgrants 官方申请页**上写的 deadline 为准。citeturn0search1  

另外，这一季还加入了一个 **AI 评估器**：你提交后会被自动打分，跟“治理意图、超级链对齐、分发设计、OP 使用效率”这些维度对比；不够对齐的提案会直接被筛掉。citeturn0search1  

---

## 2. LiqPass 跟这个补助金的匹配度

站在 Grants Council 的脑子里看你的项目：

### 你已经满足的前置条件

1. **已上线 / 正在 Base 上运行**  
   - 你有合约、前端、测试用户，这是“已上线、准备扩张”的状态。  
   - Base 是 Superchain 里头部链，这正是他们希望项目去拉动 TVL / 费用的主战场。citeturn0search4turn0search6  

2. **有明确的经济闭环**  
   - 用户付保费 → 出现强平 → 提交证据 → 按规则赔付。  
   - 这本身就是一个**产生链上行为 + DEX / perp 交易量**的结构。

### 可以对齐的成功指标（建议主打两个）

**A. Superchain Transaction Fees（主要）**

你可以把自己定位成：

> “让本来不敢玩高杠杆的散户安心在 Base 上开合约，从而增加 perp 协议的交易额、持仓时间，进而放大 Superchain 的交易费用。”

可量化的东西包括：

- 接入某个/几个 Base perp 协议后：  
  - 受 LiqPass 影响的 **日均/周均成交量**  
  - 这些成交量对应的 **Base 链上 gas 费用**（可以用 Dune 或官方 dashboard 去估算/查询）

**B. Superchain TVL（作为加分项）**

你可以强调：

- 通过 LiqPass，用户愿意把更多资金桥到 Base / 存入 perp 协议作为保证金；  
- 对应的 **保证金 TVL** 可以纳入“TVL 贡献”。  

他们之前的分析也很看重“每 1 OP 带来多少净 TVL 流入”，所以你完全可以用“**保费补贴 → 带来的新增保证金 TVL**”这个角度去讲。citeturn4search0  

---

## 3. 申请时你要准备哪三块核心内容

你在表单里，大体会被问到三件事：**增长计划、衡量方式、资金分配**。下面我帮你把每块翻译成 LiqPass 语言，并塞一段可以直接改一改就用的英文骨架。

---

### 3.1 增长计划（Growth Plan）

建议核心叙事：

> “用可验证的‘爆仓保险’降低散户参与门槛 → 提升 Base 上 perp 协议的 **活跃用户数、开仓频次、时间加权持仓（OI）**。”

可以写成类似：

> **Growth Plan (draft)**  
> We will integrate LiqPass, a verifiable liquidation-protection layer, with one or more perp protocols on Base. Today, many retail users avoid high leverage because liquidation risk is unbounded. LiqPass lets them cap tail losses via short-term, parameterized protection (e.g. 24h / 8h covers).  
>  
> Our growth plan has three phases:  
> 1) ship a minimal integration and co-marketing campaign with a Base-native perp,  
> 2) subsidize early policies with OP so that users can try high leverage with bounded downside,  
> 3) iterate on pricing and UX based on onchain and exchange data.  
>  
> The expected impact is higher time-weighted open interest and more frequent trading activity on Base, which directly increases Superchain transaction fees.

你只需要把 “one or more perp protocols” 换成你现实里最想冲的目标（比如某个具体的 Base perp DEX）。

---

### 3.2 衡量成功的方法（Success Metrics）

表单上会问：你怎么证明自己拉动了 TVL / 费用？

可以这样设计一组 **对 OP 友好的指标**：

1. **Superchain Transaction Fees（主指标）**
   - 来自 LiqPass 用户的：  
     - 总交易笔数  
     - 总成交额（USD）  
     - 估算对应的 **gas 费用贡献**（用官方或 Dune 统计）  
2. **TVL / 保证金维度**
   - LiqPass 用户平均保证金余额  
   - 因保费补贴带来的 **新增净保证金 TVL**（对比控制组 / 历史基线）  
3. **产品自身健康度**
   - 保费收入（USD）  
   - 赔付支出 / 损失率（Loss Ratio）  
   - 有效保单数、活跃用户数

可以写成英文骨架：

> **Success Metrics (draft)**  
> We will report impact primarily through:  
> - Incremental trading volume (USD) and number of trades generated by LiqPass protected accounts on Base.  
> - Estimated sequencer fees (gas paid) attributable to this activity, using public Superchain analytics.  
> - Net new margin TVL brought onto Base by LiqPass users during the campaign window.  
> - LiqPass product health metrics (premium volume, claims count, loss ratio) to ensure the design is sustainable.  
>  
> Wherever possible, we will benchmark against a pre-campaign baseline and, if data allows, against a control group of similar users without LiqPass protection.

---

### 3.3 资金分配（Budget / OP Use）

这里他们非常关心两个问题：

1. **OP 主要是不是“用户激励 / 生态激励”？**（而不是全拿去发工资）  
2. **你预期每 1 OP 能带来大约多少 TVL / 交易费用？**citeturn4search0turn5search0  

你可以做一个很简单、但对路的拆法，比如：

- X% 用来 **补贴保费**（让用户第一次用 LiqPass 接近 0 成本）  
- Y% 用在 **跟 perp 协议的联合活动 / 排行榜奖励**  
- Z% 少量预留给 **开发 & 运营**（比如链上分析、前端改版、风控脚本）

英文骨架可以是：

> **Budget & OP Allocation (draft)**  
> We request X OP for a focused 3–6 month growth campaign on Base.  
> - ~60–70% of the OP will be used as user-facing incentives: policy subsidies and trading campaigns that require users to open and maintain positions on Base perps while protected by LiqPass.  
> - ~20–30% will be reserved for co-marketing and joint incentives with partner protocols (e.g. leaderboard rewards, campaign integrations).  
> - The remaining 10% or less will cover engineering and analytics work required to implement the integration, track Superchain TVL / fees impact, and iteratively adjust parameters.  
>  
> Our goal is to achieve at least $Y of net Superchain TVL inflows or $Z in additional transaction fees per OP distributed, in line with or above prior Growth Grants benchmarks.

Y / Z 可以先按保守值写，后面我们再一起算一版更严谨的“目标 ROI”。

---

## 4. 你现在可以直接做的下一步

不绕弯子，接下来你可以按这个顺序推进：

1. **确认链选择：**  
   - 在申请页面上，选 **Base** 作为主要目标链（Superchain 生态里 Base 本身就是增长重点）。citeturn0search4turn0search6  

2. **把上面三块内容整理成英文草稿**：  
   - Growth Plan  
   - Success Metrics  
   - Budget & OP Allocation  

3. **在 opgrants 后台创建/登录账号，选 Growth Grants 轨道**  
   - 把 LiqPass 定义成“帮助 perp 协议提升交易量和 TVL、同时可验证、可审计的风险缓冲层”，所有叙事都往“拉 TVL / 拉费用”上拧。

等你准备好想提交的版本，我们就可以直接对着具体字段做一轮“AI 审稿人视角”的改写，把话术压到更利于通过筛选的形态，让这条 Growth Grants 线，变成你 Arbitrum + Base + Hadron 之外的又一个资金支点。

### Your project / idea*  
您的项目/想法 *

> **LiqPass is parametric liquidation insurance for high-leverage traders, starting with BTC/USDC and BTC/USDT perpetuals.**  
>  
> Today, a small retail trader who uses 20–100x leverage faces one simple problem: a single liquidation can wipe out their entire account. Exchanges offer “insurance funds” for systemic risk, but there is almost no product that lets an individual trader pre-define a maximum loss for a specific position in a transparent, verifiable way.  
>  
> LiqPass lets a trader buy a short-term “liquidation cover” (for example 8h / 24h / 30-day or a max drawdown policy) linked to a specific position. If a liquidation or pre-defined drawdown event happens within the coverage window, the trader receives a fixed or formula-based payout.  
>  
> Technically, the system is event-driven and evidence-first:  
> - **On-chain**: a Solana program that issues policies, receives premiums, and holds a capped payout treasury.  
> - **Off-chain but verifiable**: a read-only verifier that ingests liquidation events and PnL data from integrated perps venues, normalizes them, builds a hash tree, and anchors a root or signature on-chain for auditability.  
>  
> On Solana, we will:  
> - Build native programs for premium collection, policy issuance (SBT/NFT-style receipts) and parametric payouts.  
> - Integrate with one or more Solana perp/derivatives protocols to use their position/liq data as the event source.  
> - Open-source the core verification and proof tooling so other teams can build their own parametric risk products on top.  

**中文理解版：**一句话就是：做“高杠杆合约的爆仓保险”，在 Solana 上变成一套原生参数化赔付基础设施，事件驱动、证据优先。

---

资金金额 *

>  
> We are requesting USD 40,000 in funding (or the equivalent in SOL), allocated across protocol development, verification infra, and a focused Solana pilot with early users.

---

### Budget proposal*  
预算提案 *

> We plan to use the grant to ship both **Solana-native protocol components** and a **real user pilot**.  
>  
> **Milestone 1 – Solana core protocol (3 months) – USD 15,000**  
> - Design and deploy the first Solana program for:  
>   - Premium collection in SOL/USDC.  
>   - Policy issuance as non-transferable receipts (SBT-style or constrained NFTs).  
>   - Capped payout logic with daily risk budgets.  
> - Deliverables: audited program repo (internal or community review), basic SDK for TypeScript, end-to-end localnet + devnet test suite.  
> - KPI examples: one program ID live on devnet and mainnet, ≥ 10 end-to-end test scenarios passing (liquidation + no-liquidation paths).  
>  
> **Milestone 2 – Verification & proof infra (2 months) – USD 10,000**  
> - Build a read-only verifier that can:  
>   - Ingest normalized position and liquidation events from at least one Solana perp/derivatives protocol.  
>   - Construct a Merkle tree or equivalent hash bundle for each evidence package.  
>   - Anchor a root or signed digest on-chain for transparent audits.  
> - Deliverables: open-source verifier service, evidence format spec, CLI tools for replay and verification.  
> - KPI examples: ability to replay and verify at least 1 month of historical liq events for the integrated protocol in a reproducible way.  
>  
> **Milestone 3 – Solana pilot & early user adoption (2 months) – USD 8,000**  
> - Integrate LiqPass with a Solana perp venue in a basic but usable flow (e.g. link a position to a 24h cover).  
> - Run a limited pilot with real traders, subsidizing a small number of policies to observe behavior and risk metrics.  
> - Deliverables: pilot UI, operational runbook, pilot report (loss ratio, user feedback, protocol feedback).  
> - KPI examples: policies purchased during pilot, percentage of policies that trigger payouts, qualitative feedback from traders and the partner protocol.  
>  
> **Milestone 4 – Public goods: docs, SDKs, dashboards (1–2 months) – USD 7,000**  
> - Produce detailed documentation and reference integrations for other Solana teams.  
> - Ship an open-source TypeScript SDK and simple UI components that any protocol can embed.  
> - Publish a basic public dashboard showing risk metrics (e.g. liquidation probabilities and realized loss ratios) for the integrated markets.  
> - KPI examples: docs online, SDK integrated by at least 1 external team, public dashboard tracking at least one perp market.  

**中文要点：**  
4 个里程碑：1）Solana 合约；2）验证 & 证据基础设施；3）Solana 上首个小规模试点；4）作为公共产品的文档、SDK、看板。金额加起来 40k。

---

### Relevant metrics about the usage of your project/product*  
关于您的项目/产品使用情况的相关指标 *

> We are **pre-launch on Solana**, but the underlying product already exists as a working MVP on another chain:  
>  
> - A simplified premium-collection contract is already deployed on-chain and integrated with a live front-end to sell short-term “liquidation cover” policies in USDC.  
> - We have a functional multi-service architecture:  
>   - A checkout backend that records policies and emits verifiable events.  
>   - A separate verification service that connects to centralized exchanges via read-only APIs to reconstruct liquidation events and PnL.  
> - We have run multiple internal test cycles with real historical BTC perp data to calibrate liquidation probabilities and payout caps for high-leverage scenarios (e.g. 20–100x).  
> - A small closed group of early traders has been testing the UX and risk parameters in a private alpha environment, giving us feedback on product design and language.  
>  
> The Solana grant would fund the port of this architecture into a native Solana implementation plus a focused pilot with early users on Solana perps.

**中文：**不夸大“已经有大量用户”，强调：已有一套在别链跑起来的 MVP + 内部真实数据测试 + 少量早期用户测试，Solana 还在 pre-launch。

---

### What best describes the funding status of your project?*  
您的项目目前的资金状况最符合以下哪项描述？*

> The project is **bootstrapped / pre-seed**.  
>  
> It is currently founder-funded with no VC, no token sale, and no prior ecosystem grants. We are specifically looking for non-dilutive, ecosystem-aligned grants (such as this Solana Foundation grant) to fund the protocol and public-goods tooling, while keeping the core economics conservative and sustainable.

（如果你之后真的拿到了别的链的 grant，可以把这段更新一下。）

---

### Why you?*  
为什么是你？*

> I am building LiqPass as a founder who has lived both sides of the problem:  
>  
> - As a high-leverage retail trader, I have personally experienced how one liquidation can erase months of work, and how little tooling exists to cap downside in a simple, transparent way.  
> - As a builder, I have already shipped a cross-region, multi-service MVP: smart contracts, backends, verification services, and a front-end UI that all speak the same “evidence-first” language.  
>  
> My edge is a combination of **deep focus on a narrow problem** (liquidation risk for high-leverage traders) and an **obsession with verifiability**:  
>  
> - All critical flows are designed to be reproducible from raw data: read-only exchange APIs or on-chain events → normalized records → hash trees → verifiable claims.  
> - The architecture is intentionally minimal and modular so that a small team (or even a solo founder plus contributors) can maintain it safely.  
>  
> On Solana specifically, I am not trying to “boil the ocean” or build a generalized insurance marketplace from day one. The goal is to ship one focused, verifiable risk primitive for perps traders, prove that it works with real users, and then open it up as a building block that other Solana teams can reuse.

**中文要点：**  
- 你自己既是高杠杆散户，又是这套系统从 0 到 1 的唯一总工；  
- 优势在于：专注一个窄问题 + 极度重视“可验证、可复算”的架构；  
- 在 Solana 上不搞大而全，而是把一个“爆仓风险积木”打磨好。

---

### Competition*  
谁是您项目的直接竞争对手？*

> On the DeFi side, the closest competitors are **generalized on-chain insurance / risk protocols**, such as:  
> - Mutual-style insurance protocols that cover protocol hacks or exchange defaults.  
> - Automated coverage protocols that underwrite smart-contract or bridge risk.  
>  
> These are important, but they typically **do not** offer:  
> - Very short-term, position-linked covers (8h / 24h).  
> - Parametric liquidation triggers tied to a specific perp position.  
> - A workflow that is reproducible from raw position and liquidation data for individual traders.  
>  
> On the centralized side, major exchanges operate **insurance funds**, but these are opaque, collective pools designed to backstop system-wide losses, not to give individual traders a clear, pre-priced “max loss” on a specific position.  
>  
> LiqPass is deliberately narrower: we specialise in **micro-duration, position-level liquidation cover** with transparent, verifiable triggers. The Solana implementation makes this primitive available natively to Solana perps venues and other builders.

**中文：**  
竞品分两类：DeFi 保险协议（大而泛，偏黑客/合约风险），以及 CEX 的“保险基金”（系统性兜底）。LiqPass 的差异：只做“短期、单笔仓位、参数化爆仓赔付”。

---

### How is this project a public good?*  
这个项目如何体现公共利益？*

> We plan to structure the Solana work so that a significant part of it is a **public good for the ecosystem**:  
>  
> 1. **Open-source Solana programs and SDKs**  
>    - The core Solana programs for premium collection, policy receipts, and parametric payouts will be open-sourced under a permissive license, together with a TypeScript SDK and example front-end components.  
>    - Any Solana perp, aggregator, or wallet can integrate these building blocks to offer their own risk products without having to rebuild the core logic.  
>  
> 2. **Open verification and evidence tooling**  
>    - The evidence formats, hash-tree construction, and replay tools will be open-source so that anyone can independently verify whether a payout was triggered correctly.  
>    - Other teams can reuse this tooling to build their own parametric products (not only liquidation insurance).  
>  
> 3. **Free community offering: risk insights and education**  
>    - We will maintain a free public dashboard that shows liquidation probabilities, realized loss ratios, and other risk metrics for supported markets on Solana.  
>    - This helps traders and protocol teams better understand leverage risk, even if they never buy a policy from LiqPass.  
>  
> Even if the LiqPass business layer remains partially proprietary (for example, certain risk limits or capital management policies), the **Solana programs, SDKs, verification tools, and risk data** are designed to be reusable public goods that lower the cost of building robust, evidence-driven risk primitives in the Solana ecosystem.

**中文要点：**  
- 合约 + SDK + 组件开源；  
- 证据工具链开源，可复算；  
- 免费公开的风险看板，给不用你产品的人也带来信息价值；  
- 商业层可以部分私有，但底层积木是 Solana 生态的公共基础设施。

---

LiqPass (“爆仓保”) is a parametric liquidation cover for high-leverage perp traders. Users pay a small fixed premium to cap their worst-case loss over a short window (e.g. 8h / 24h), while all triggers and payouts are driven by verifiable on-chain events and read-only exchange data. Our core mission is to make high-leverage trading safer and more predictable for retail users, and to help Sei-based trading protocols grow volume and fee income by reducing users’ fear of liquidation.

LiqPass（“爆仓保”）是一款面向高杠杆合约交易者的参数化“爆仓保险”。用户只需支付一笔小额固定保费，就可以在 8 小时 / 24 小时等短周期内，为自己的最坏亏损设定一个上限，触发与赔付严格依托链上事件和交易所只读数据进行判定。我们的核心使命是：在不改变交易规则的前提下，让散户参与高杠杆交易更安全、更可控，同时帮助部署在 Sei 上的交易协议提升交易量和手续费收入。

<!-- mid: c449d84b-1063-44b2-afad-52ed49161bb5 -->
Most retail perp traders on Sei (and other chains) use very high leverage with small collateral, so a single wick or short-term volatility spike can wipe out their account. Once they “get liquidated hard” a few times, they quit, reducing long-term volume and fee income for protocols. Existing tools (stop-loss, take-profit, risk popups) help, but they don’t *cap* worst-case loss, and most “insurance funds” are opaque, centralized, or require manual, subjective claims. LiqPass solves this by turning liquidation risk into a transparent, fixed-premium cover: users pay a small premium to cap tail losses in a short time window, and payouts are driven by deterministic, verifiable rules instead of human discretion.

大多数使用 Sei 等链上永续合约的散户，都是“小本金 + 高杠杆”，稍微遇到一根极端 K 线或短时波动，就可能被强平，一两次“爆得很惨”之后就不再参与，导致协议很难沉淀长期用户和稳定交易量。现有工具（止损、提醒、风控弹窗等）只能缓解风险，却不能真正“封顶最坏亏损”；而市面上的“保险基金”要么不透明、要么中心化、要么依赖人工审核和主观判断。LiqPass 通过参数化的爆仓保障，把这种尾部风险变成一笔小而确定的保费：用户为短周期支付固定保费，即可把最坏亏损锁在一个上限内，触发与赔付交由可验证的规则自动判定，而不是人为拍脑袋。

<!-- mid: 5eacbe3a-406d-4da2-b625-0223a8123953 -->
明白，你说得对：**现在 Sei 上还没有成熟的合约 DEX / 永续交易者生态**，所以不能把“问题”写成“Sei 上的合约散户怎样怎样”。

我们换个角度，从「链的繁荣 + 稳定币 + 交易量 + 用户留存」这个层面来写，一次性给你一版可直接贴到表单里的中英双语答案：

---

**EN**

Today, most high-leverage trading by retail users happens off-chain on centralized exchanges, while L1 ecosystems like Sei mainly see passive holding and simple transfers. This creates two gaps:

1. **For users** – there is no simple, transparent way to cap downside risk on their high-volatility trading. A few bad liquidations make them churn and stop participating altogether.  
2. **For the chain** – stablecoins and trading volume stay off-chain, and there is no “risk layer” infrastructure that turns short-term trading activity into long-term on-chain relationships and flows.

LiqPass solves this by using Sei as the **settlement and proof layer** for liquidation / drawdown protection. Premiums, guarantees, and payouts are all settled in stablecoins on Sei, with verifiable rules instead of trusted intermediaries.  

For Sei, this creates a new public good primitive: a reusable, chain-level “risk buffer” that can plug into future perps, DEXs, or structured products, bringing more stablecoin volume on-chain, smoothing user PnL, and improving user retention across the ecosystem.

---

**中**

目前，大部分散户的高杠杆交易都发生在中心化交易所，像 Sei 这样的公链，链上更多只是**被动持币、简单转账**，缺少与这些高波动交易场景直接相关的基础设施，导致两类问题：

1. **对用户而言**：没有一个简单、透明的方式，把高波动交易的最坏亏损“封顶”。几次爆仓之后，就干脆不玩了，长期参与意愿很差。  
2. **对公链生态而言**：稳定币和真实交易量留在链下，链上缺少“风险缓冲层”这种基础设施，无法把短期的交易行为沉淀为长期的链上关系和资金流。

LiqPass 把 Sei 用作**清结算与证明层**：对“爆仓 / 大回撤”的保障，在 Sei 链上用稳定币结算保费、承诺与赔付，并通过可验证规则而不是人工裁量来执行。  

对于 Sei 而言，这相当于新增了一种“公共风险缓冲”原语，可以在未来对接永续合约、DEX 或结构化产品，把更多稳定币交易活动引到链上，平滑用户收益波动，提升整个生态的用户留存与资金利用效率。

---

你可以整段粘贴；如果字数太紧，就删掉一些“未来对接 XX 协议”的句子，保留核心逻辑：**链下高杠杆风险 → 链上稳定币结算 + 可验证保障 → 带来交易量、留存和生态粘性**。

按回车键 ↵   # LiqPass – Liquidation Insurance for Leveraged Traders on BNB Chain

## 1. Problem: Retail Leverage Is a One-Strike Game

- Small-account retail traders on CEXs are afraid to use high leverage.  
- A single forced liquidation can wipe out their entire account; there is no simple, budgeted loss cap.  
- Communities, KOLs, and quant teams want to bring users into perps, but lack a reusable, verifiable protection module.  
- For exchanges and chains, this means lost volume, lost stablecoin TVL, and short-lived user cohorts.

---

## 2. Solution: LiqPass (爆仓保)

**A parametric liquidation-insurance layer for leveraged traders, designed to sit between Binance and BNB Chain.**

- Users pay a small stablecoin premium (on BNB Chain) for short-term coverage (8h / 24h / monthly).  
- If a forced liquidation happens within the coverage window, they submit the Binance order ID.  
- LiqPass passively verifies the event via Binance read-only APIs and an off-chain evidence pipeline.  
- The evidence bundle (normalized data + hash) is anchored on-chain; valid events trigger rule-based, capped payouts.  
- Everything is event-driven and auditable: no opaque risk decisions, no ad-hoc screenshots.

---

## 3. Why It Matters for Binance & BNB Chain

**For retail users**

- Gives small traders a clear, budgeted loss cap instead of unlimited downside.  
- Turns “爆仓恐惧” into a known, up-front premium they can afford.  
- Makes high-leverage strategies survivable instead of a one-shot gamble.

**For Binance & BNB Chain**

- Attracts and retains small-account leverage users who would otherwise never enter.  
- Increases perp volume and fee revenue as users trade and hold positions more confidently.  
- Keeps stablecoins (USDT / USDC and BNB-native stables) circulating on BNB Chain.  
- Creates a new fee-sharing line: insurance premiums can be shared among Binance, LiqPass, and ecosystem partners.

---

## 4. Product Snapshot (Today)

- **MVP already running on another chain**
  - Checkout contract deployed on mainnet, emitting premium payment events.  
  - Backend + verifier service integrated with CEX read-only APIs.  
  - Web app flows for: buy coverage → see active policies → submit claims.

- **Evidence-first design**
  - Each claim is backed by normalized order data + derived metrics.  
  - Evidence → Merkle/root hash → anchored on-chain for auditability.

- This grant / partnership will be used to **port and specialize LiqPass for Binance & BNB Chain**.

![image-20251123165637479](./${img}/image-20251123165637479.png)

![image-20251123165657141](./${img}/image-20251123165657141.png)

![image-20251123170114351](./${img}/image-20251123170114351.png)

---

## 5. How It Works (High Level)

1. **Buy coverage on BNB Chain**  
   User connects wallet, selects a product (e.g. 24h BTCUSDT 50x coverage), and pays a stablecoin premium.

2. **Wait & trade on Binance**  
   User trades on Binance as usual with leverage; LiqPass only listens via read-only APIs.

3. **If liquidation happens**  
   User submits the Binance order ID to LiqPass.

4. **Verification & evidence**  
   Off-chain service fetches order/trade history from Binance APIs, normalizes it, builds an evidence bundle, and signs a result.

5. **On-chain decision & payout**  
   BNB Chain contracts check coverage window + rules + evidence hash; if valid, they trigger capped payouts in stablecoins.

Everything is driven by objective events and reproducible data—no discretionary “claims handling”.

---

## 6. Business Model & Unit Economics

**Revenue streams**

- Retail premiums: users pay for 8h / 24h / monthly coverage.  
- Revenue sharing: Binance / BNB perp partners / communities receive a share of premiums.  
- (Optional) Trading fee rebates: additional upside if integrated with volume programs.

**Risk & margins (targets)**

- Target loss ratio: ~60–70% (payouts / premiums).  
- Target gross margin: ~20–30% after risk load, ops, and partner share.  
- Risk controls: per-user and per-day caps, max payout as % of notional, waiting periods, blacklists.

---

## 7. Roadmap for Binance / BNB Chain

**Phase 1 – BNB Chain integration (0–3 months)**

- Port core liquidation-insurance logic to BNB Smart Chain.  
- Deploy initial contracts for policy storage, events, and payouts.  
- Connect to Binance read-only APIs in a BNB-focused verifier service.

**Phase 2 – Public beta (3–6 months)**

- Launch a public beta dApp on BNB Chain for retail users.  
- Offer a small set of focused products (e.g. BTCUSDT 20x / 50x 24h coverage).  
- Start with conservative limits and transparent reporting.

**Phase 3 – Deep integration & scaling (6–12 months)**

- Integrate LiqPass as an embedded module with selected Binance products / partners.  
- Expand coverage to more markets and user segments.  
- Optimize pricing and risk parameters using real-world data.

---

## 8. Why Me (Founder)

- Solo founder with full-stack ownership: frontend, backend, smart contracts, and infra.  
- Prior attempts taught me to focus on **small, verifiable loops** instead of grand, vague roadmaps.  
- I already shipped a working MVP on another chain with minimal resources.  
- Committed to turning risk management into open, auditable infrastructure—not a black box.

Handles & links:

- GitHub: https://github.com/wjz5788/LiqPass  
- Demo: https://wjz5788.com/  
- X (Twitter): @CryptoNaxin  
- Telegram: https://t.me/mosheng1u  

---

## 9. What We’re Asking From Binance

- Strategic investment (equity or token-based) to scale LiqPass as a dedicated protection layer.  
- Product and technical collaboration to embed LiqPass-style protection into Binance / BNB experiences.  
- Support on risk, data, and go-to-market so we can bring more users, more stablecoins, and more sustainable volume into the BNB ecosystem.

> “Give retail a survivable way to trade leverage; give Binance and BNB Chain a new, verifiable risk primitive.”

---

## 10. Claims & Treasury Safety (24h Settlement)

- LiqPass uses a 24h settlement window for claims instead of unconstrained instant payouts.  
- Same-day accounting, next-day settlement: approved claims are recorded immediately, paid in a 24h batch.  
- Smart contracts focus on recording, limits, and emitting events; they do **not** allow unlimited withdrawals.  
- Per-day / per-user / per-policy caps plus circuit breakers bound worst-case damage to a single settlement cycle.  
- A transparency dashboard shows 7D policies sold, loss ratio, treasury balance, daily premium vs. paid, and full event logs.  
- Even if there is a contract or logic bug, the treasury cannot be drained in one shot; the system can be paused and fixed.

下面是已经改成 **Sei 版本** 的整份 pitch 文本，你可以直接拿去做文档 / Notion / PDF，然后把链接填到表单里。

---

# LiqPass – Liquidation Insurance for Leveraged Traders, Settled on Sei

## 1. Problem: Retail Leverage Is a One-Strike Game

- Small-account retail traders on CEXs are afraid to use high leverage.  
- A single forced liquidation can wipe out their entire account; there is no simple, budgeted loss cap.  
- Communities, KOLs, and quant teams want to bring users into perps, but lack a reusable, verifiable protection module.  
- For chains like Sei, most on-chain activity is still holding / simple transfers; the risk and PnL from leveraged trading largely stay off-chain, along with the users and stablecoin flows.

---

## 2. Solution: LiqPass (爆仓保)

**A parametric liquidation-insurance layer for leveraged traders, using Sei as the on-chain settlement and proof layer between CEX perps and the L1.**

- Users pay a small stablecoin premium (on Sei) for short-term coverage (8h / 24h / monthly).  
- If a forced liquidation happens within the coverage window, they submit the order ID from a supported perp venue (e.g. Binance).  
- LiqPass passively verifies the event via read-only exchange APIs and an off-chain evidence pipeline.  
- The evidence bundle (normalized data + hash) is anchored on Sei; valid events trigger rule-based, capped payouts in stablecoins.  
- Everything is event-driven and auditable: no opaque risk decisions, no ad-hoc screenshots.

---

## 3. Why It Matters for the Sei Ecosystem

**For retail users**

- Gives small traders a clear, budgeted loss cap instead of unlimited downside.  
- Turns “爆仓恐惧” into a known, up-front premium they can afford.  
- Makes high-leverage strategies survivable instead of a one-shot gamble, while keeping settlement transparent and on-chain.

**For Sei and its ecosystem**

- Pulls part of the risk, settlement, and user relationship of leveraged trading **onto Sei**, even if the actual trading is still happening on CEXs or other perps.  
- Brings more stablecoin flows and active wallets onto Sei via premiums, claim payouts, and future protocol integrations.  
- Creates a reusable “risk buffer” primitive that future Sei-native perps, DEXs, and structured products can plug into.  
- Aligns with Sei’s vision as a high-performance, trading-focused L1 by adding a missing layer: transparent, programmable risk management.

---

## 4. Product Snapshot (Today)

- **MVP already running on another chain**
  - Checkout contract deployed on mainnet, emitting premium payment events.  
  - Backend + verifier service integrated with CEX read-only APIs.  
  - Web app flows for: buy coverage → see active policies → submit claims.

- **Evidence-first design**
  - Each claim is backed by normalized order data + derived metrics.  
  - Evidence → Merkle/root hash → anchored on-chain for auditability.

- This grant / partnership will be used to **port and specialize LiqPass for Sei** as the settlement and proof layer.

![image-20251123165637479](./${img}/image-20251123165637479.png)

![image-20251123165657141](./${img}/image-20251123165657141.png)

![image-20251123170114351](./${img}/image-20251123170114351.png)

---

## 5. How It Works (High Level)

1. **Buy coverage on Sei**  
   User connects wallet to Sei, selects a product (e.g. 24h BTCUSDT 50x coverage), and pays a stablecoin premium on-chain.

2. **Wait & trade on perps**  
   User trades on supported perp venues (e.g. Binance CEX today, Sei-native perps in the future); LiqPass only listens via read-only APIs.

3. **If liquidation happens**  
   User submits the order ID from the perp venue to LiqPass.

4. **Verification & evidence**  
   Off-chain service fetches order/trade history from the venue’s APIs, normalizes it, builds an evidence bundle, and signs a result.

5. **On-chain decision & payout**  
   Sei contracts check coverage window + rules + evidence hash; if valid, they trigger capped payouts in stablecoins.

Everything is driven by objective events and reproducible data—no discretionary “claims handling”.

---

## 6. Business Model & Unit Economics

**Revenue streams**

- Retail premiums: users pay for 8h / 24h / monthly coverage.  
- Revenue sharing: Sei ecosystem partners (perps, DEXs, communities) can receive a share of premiums.  
- (Optional) Trading fee rebates: additional upside if integrated with exchange or protocol volume programs.

**Risk & margins (targets)**

- Target loss ratio: ~60–70% (payouts / premiums).  
- Target gross margin: ~20–30% after risk load, ops, and partner share.  
- Risk controls: per-user and per-day caps, max payout as % of notional, waiting periods, blacklists.

---

## 7. Roadmap for Sei

**Phase 1 – Sei integration (0–3 months)**

- Port core liquidation-insurance logic to Sei smart contracts.  
- Deploy initial contracts for policy storage, events, and payouts on Sei.  
- Run a verifier service that talks to CEX APIs and anchors signed results on Sei.

**Phase 2 – Public beta (3–6 months)**

- Launch a public beta dApp on Sei for retail users.  
- Offer a small set of focused products (e.g. BTCUSDT 20x / 50x 24h coverage) settled in Sei-based stablecoins.  
- Start with conservative limits and transparent reporting.

**Phase 3 – Deep integration & scaling (6–12 months)**

- Integrate LiqPass as an embedded module with future Sei-native perp protocols, DEXs, and structured products.  
- Expand coverage to more markets and user segments.  
- Optimize pricing and risk parameters using real-world data from Sei and connected venues.

---

## 8. Why Me (Founder)

- Solo founder with full-stack ownership: frontend, backend, smart contracts, and infra.  
- Prior attempts taught me to focus on **small, verifiable loops** instead of grand, vague roadmaps.  
- I already shipped a working MVP on another chain with minimal resources.  
- Committed to turning risk management into open, auditable infrastructure—not a black box.

Handles & links:

- GitHub: https://github.com/wjz5788/LiqPass  
- Demo: https://wjz5788.com/  
- X (Twitter): @CryptoNaxin  
- Telegram: https://t.me/mosheng1u  

---

## 9. What We’re Asking From the Sei Foundation

- Grant funding to port and operate LiqPass as a public-good risk layer on Sei.  
- Product and technical collaboration to plug LiqPass-style protection into future Sei-based trading protocols.  
- Ecosystem support and visibility so we can onboard early users, partners, and design an open risk dashboard for the Sei community.

> “Give retail a survivable way to trade leverage; give Sei a new, verifiable risk primitive.”

---

## 10. Claims & Treasury Safety (24h Settlement)

- LiqPass uses a 24h settlement window for claims instead of unconstrained instant payouts.  
- Same-day accounting, next-day settlement: approved claims are recorded immediately, paid in a 24h batch.  
- Sei smart contracts focus on recording, limits, and emitting events; they do **not** allow unlimited withdrawals.  
- Per-day / per-user / per-policy caps plus circuit breakers bound worst-case damage to a single settlement cycle.  
- A transparency dashboard shows 7D policies sold, loss ratio, treasury balance, daily premium vs. paid, and full event logs.  
- Even if there is a contract or logic bug, the treasury cannot be drained in one shot; the system can be paused and fixed.

![image-20251123170212988](./${img}/image-20251123170212988.png)

---

这版已经把 Binance / BNB Chain 全部换成了「CEX + Sei 结算层」的叙事，重点放在：**把链下高杠杆风险结算拉到 Sei 上，带来稳定币流量和用户留存**。你只需要把它放进一个文档（Notion、Google Docs、GitHub Pages 都行），生成链接，贴回申请表即可。

---

**EN**

Sei is positioning itself as a high-performance, trading-focused L1 that optimizes for latency, throughput, and capital efficiency. LiqPass fits into this vision as a **risk and settlement primitive** that makes trading on Sei more sustainable for normal users and more valuable for protocols.

- **More sticky stablecoin flows** – LiqPass settles premiums and payouts in stablecoins on Sei, creating recurring on-chain flows that are directly tied to trading behavior (not just airdrops or yield campaigns).  
- **Better user retention for trading apps** – by giving retail users a clear, budgeted loss cap, Sei-native perp / DEX protocols can keep users trading through drawdowns instead of rage-quitting after a few bad liquidations.  
- **Composable infrastructure** – LiqPass is designed as a reusable module: future Sei perps, structured products, and even social-trading apps can plug into the same verification + payout layer instead of rebuilding their own opaque “insurance funds”.  
- **Data-driven, transparent risk** – all decisions are based on verifiable events and on-chain proofs, which aligns with Sei’s push for transparent, programmable trading infrastructure rather than black-box CeFi risk.

In short, LiqPass turns high-volatility trading activity into **durable on-chain relationships, stablecoin volume, and protocol revenue** on Sei.

---

**中**

Sei 正在把自己定位为一个**为交易而优化的高性能公链**，重点是低延迟、高吞吐和更高的资金效率。LiqPass 则作为一层**风控与结算基础设施**，让“在 Sei 上做交易”对普通用户更可持续、对协议更有价值。

- **带来更粘性的稳定币流量**：LiqPass 在 Sei 上结算保费和赔付，这些稳定币流动是与真实交易行为强绑定的，而不是一次性的空投或短期挖矿活动。  
- **提升交易类应用的用户留存**：通过给散户一个明确、可预算的最坏亏损上限，Sei 上未来的永续合约 / DEX 协议可以在用户经历回撤时把人“留在桌边”，而不是在几次爆仓后彻底流失。  
- **作为可组合的基础模块**：LiqPass 从设计上就是一个可复用的模块，未来在 Sei 上的合约交易、结构化产品、跟单 / 社交交易等，都可以复用同一套“验证 + 赔付”层，而不是各自搞一个不透明的保险池。  
- **数据驱动、可审计的风险管理**：所有判断都基于可验证事件和链上锚定的证据，而非中心化黑箱，与 Sei 打造透明、可编程交易基础设施的方向天然一致。

一句话总结：LiqPass 把高波动的交易行为，转化为 **Sei 链上的稳定币体量、协议收入和长期用户关系**。

直接写「我们具体用了 Sei 的哪些特性」，别讲大空话。下面一段可以直接贴到表单（中英都有）：

---

**EN**

LiqPass uses Sei as the **high-performance settlement and proof layer** for liquidation protection:

- **High throughput & low latency** – Premium payments, policy creation, and payout events are all on-chain, so fast finality on Sei keeps the user experience close to “CEX speed” even though settlement is on L1.  
- **Trading-optimized architecture** – LiqPass is designed to plug into Sei’s trading ecosystem (perps, DEXs, structured products). Protocols can call our contracts to offer one-click protection, while we rely on Sei’s ability to handle frequent, bursty order flows.  
- **Composable contracts & data** – All policies, payouts, and evidence hashes are stored on Sei, so other Sei protocols can read and build on top of this data (e.g. risk dashboards, vaults that underwrite LiqPass, structured products that bundle coverage).  
- **Capital efficiency** – By settling in stablecoins on Sei and using capped, parametric payouts, we can keep capital requirements lower and reuse the same on-chain treasury across multiple products and partners.

In short, LiqPass treats Sei as the **place where risk is recorded, priced, and settled** for leveraged trading.

---

**中**

LiqPass 把 Sei 当作**清结算与证明层**来用，把它的“为交易优化”的能力直接用在风控产品上：

- **高吞吐 + 低延迟**：保费支付、保单创建、赔付事件全部上链，在 Sei 上的快速出块和确认，让用户在用链上产品时，体感更接近 CEX，而不是“点一下等半天”。  
- **围绕交易场景的架构**：LiqPass 未来可以直接嵌入 Sei 上的永续合约、DEX、结构化产品中，让协议通过合约调用一键为用户附加“爆仓保护”，同时利用 Sei 处理高频、突发交易流量的能力。  
- **可组合的合约与数据**：所有保单、赔付记录和证据哈希都写在 Sei 上，其他 Sei 协议可以直接读取这些数据，做风险看板、做为 LiqPass 提供承保资金的金库，或者把保障打包进新的结构化产品里。  
- **资金效率**：在 Sei 上用稳定币结算、采用有上限的参数化赔付模型，可以让所需准备金更可控，同一笔链上资金池可以为多种产品、多家合作方提供保障。

一句话：LiqPass 把 Sei 当成**记录风险、定价风险、结算风险的地方**，把链下高波动交易的“结果”，都落在 Sei 链上。

**EN**

Our roadmap for LiqPass on Sei over the next 6–12 months is:

- **Month 0–2 — Sei integration MVP**
  - Port core contracts to Sei (policy registry, premium payments, capped payouts).  
  - Stand up a Sei-connected backend + verifier that talks to CEX read-only APIs.  
  - Release an internal MVP dApp on Sei testnet: buy coverage → submit order ID → see verification result.  

- **Month 2–4 — Mainnet pilot on Sei**
  - Deploy to Sei mainnet with **1–2 narrow products** (e.g. BTC 24h coverage, conservative limits).  
  - Run a small invite-only pilot with real users and real stablecoins.  
  - Ship a basic public dashboard: policies sold, payouts, treasury balance, loss ratio.  

- **Month 4–6 — Open beta + first integrations**
  - Open LiqPass on Sei to all users with higher but still bounded limits.  
  - Integrate with at least **one Sei-native trading protocol** (perps / DEX / structured product) for one-click protection.  
  - Iterate pricing and risk parameters using real data from Sei.  

- **Month 6–12 — Hardening and scaling**
  - Add more coverage SKUs (time windows / leverage bands / assets) as data allows.  
  - Introduce an on-chain underwriting / treasury module for partners and LPs.  
  - Prepare and execute a security review / audit for long-term scaling on Sei.

---

**中**

接下来 6–12 个月，LiqPass 在 Sei 上的路线图大致是：

- **第 0–2 个月：Sei 集成 MVP**
  - 将核心合约迁移到 Sei（保单注册、保费支付、封顶赔付逻辑）。  
  - 搭建与 Sei 对接的后端 + 验证服务，对接 CEX 只读 API。  
  - 在 Sei 测试网跑通一条完整闭环：购买保障 → 提交订单号 → 返回验证结果。  

- **第 2–4 个月：Sei 主网小规模试点**
  - 部署到 Sei 主网，先上线 **1–2 个窄产品**（如 BTC 24 小时保障，额度保守）。  
  - 用真实稳定币做一轮小规模、邀请制试点。  
  - 上线基础看板：保单数量、赔付金额、资金池余额、损失率等。  

- **第 4–6 个月：开放公测 + 首个集成**
  - 在 Sei 上开放公测，适度提升额度但保持严格上限。  
  - 与至少 **一个 Sei 原生交易协议**（永续 / DEX / 结构化产品）完成集成，实现一键附加“爆仓保护”。  
  - 基于 Sei 上的真实数据，迭代定价和风控参数。  

- **第 6–12 个月：加固与扩展**
  - 在数据支撑下扩展更多 SKU（不同时间窗口 / 杠杆档位 / 标的资产）。  
  - 增加链上承保 / 资金池模块，让合作方和 LP 可以参与承担风险、分享保费。  
  - 推进安全审计和治理设计，为在 Sei 上长期扩展打基础。  

这一版既给出清晰时间表，又突出“先跑闭环、再联通协议、最后扩规模”的节奏。

  dApp on Sei, wallet flows, policy dashboards, and integration work with at least one Sei trading protocol.

- **Security review / external code review – $7,000**  
  Third-party review of core contracts + critical backend logic before scaling limits.

- **Pilot incentives & ecosystem experiments – $8,000**  
  Subsidizing initial premiums / discounts for early Sei users and partner protocols to bootstrap volume and data.

- **Reporting, analytics & basic ops – $2,000**  
  Metrics dashboard, grant reporting, basic legal/ops costs related to running the Sei pilot.

_Total: $60,000._

---

**EN**

This funding will help us deliver four concrete milestones on Sei:

1. **Milestone 1 – Sei testnet MVP live (Month 0–2)**  
   - Core LiqPass contracts deployed on Sei testnet (policy registry, premium, capped payout logic).  
   - Backend + verifier connected to CEX read-only APIs and Sei testnet.  
   - Internal dApp flow live: buy coverage → submit order ID → see verification result.  
   - **Target:** ≥ 50 test policies created, ≥ 20 successful verification flows end-to-end.

2. **Milestone 2 – Sei mainnet pilot (Month 2–4)**  
   - Deploy contracts and backend to Sei mainnet with 1–2 narrow products (e.g. BTC 24h cover).  
   - Run an invite-only pilot with real stablecoins and real users.  
   - Public dashboard showing policies sold, payouts, treasury balance, basic loss ratio.  
   - **Target:** ≥ 100 mainnet policies sold, ≥ $10,000 total premium volume during pilot.

3. **Milestone 3 – Open beta + first Sei integration (Month 4–6)**  
   - Open LiqPass on Sei to all users with conservative but higher limits.  
   - Complete at least **one integration** with a Sei-native trading protocol (perps / DEX / structured product) for one-click protection.  
   - Iterate pricing and risk parameters based on Sei on-chain and pilot data.  
   - **Target:** ≥ 300 cumulative mainnet policies, ≥ 1 live protocol integration.

4. **Milestone 4 – Hardening & risk infra (Month 6–12)**  
   - Add on-chain underwriting / treasury module for partners / LPs.  
   - Complete external security review / code audit of core contracts.  
   - Extend dashboards with more detailed risk and performance metrics for the Sei community.  
   - **Target:** audit completed, underwriting module deployed, and transparent reporting live.

---

**中**

这笔资金将主要帮助我们在 Sei 上完成以下四个可量化的里程碑：

1. **里程碑一：Sei 测试网 MVP 上线（第 0–2 个月）**  
   - 在 Sei 测试网部署核心 LiqPass 合约（保单登记、保费、封顶赔付逻辑）。  
   - 后端 + 验证服务打通 CEX 只读 API 与 Sei 测试网。  
   - dApp 跑通完整流程：购买保障 → 提交订单号 → 返回验证结果。  
   - **目标：**累计创建 ≥ 50 张测试保单，≥ 20 次完整验证闭环通过。

2. **里程碑二：Sei 主网小规模试点（第 2–4 个月）**  
   - 在 Sei 主网部署合约和后端，上线 1–2 个窄产品（如 BTC 24 小时保障）。  
   - 使用真实稳定币和真实用户，进行邀请制试点。  
   - 上线公开看板，展示保单数量、赔付金额、资金池余额、基础损失率。  
   - **目标：**主网累计售出 ≥ 100 张保单，试点期内累计保费规模 ≥ 10,000 美元。

3. **里程碑三：开放公测 + 首个 Sei 协议集成（第 4–6 个月）**  
   - 在 Sei 上对所有用户开放 LiqPass，适度提升额度但保持严格上限。  
   - 与至少 **一个 Sei 原生交易协议** 完成集成，实现一键附加“爆仓保护”。  
   - 基于 Sei 上链数据与试点数据，迭代定价和风控参数。  
   - **目标：**主网累计保单数 ≥ 300，完成 ≥ 1 个真实协议集成。

4. **里程碑四：加固与风险基础设施完善（第 6–12 个月）**  
   - 上线链上承保 / 资金池模块，引入合作方 / LP 参与承担风险和分享保费。  
   - 完成核心合约的外部安全审查 / 审计。  
   - 为 Sei 社区提供更详细的风险与表现指标看板。  
   - **目标：**审计完成，承保模块部署上线，并有可公开查看的透明报表。

这样写，评审一眼就能看到：钱下去之后，会换来**具体功能 + 明确时间点 + 可量化数据**。

<!-- mid: 8f1567e8-8aa2-497f-8bb6-fb96292655a2 -->
**EN**

Yes. The grant is meant to get LiqPass on Sei from “works in isolation” → “used by real users and protocols”, not to keep the project alive forever.

After funding, sustainability comes from three main loops:

1. **Premium-driven revenue**  
   - Users pay stablecoin premiums for short-term coverage.  
   - Each premium is split between: (a) claims treasury, (b) protocol / partner share, and (c) LiqPass operations.  
   - With a target loss ratio of ~60–70%, the remaining margin covers infra + development without needing more grants.

2. **On-chain underwriting & LP capital**  
   - We will introduce an on-chain underwriting / treasury module so partners and LPs can supply capital and earn a share of premiums.  
   - This brings external capital into the risk pool and decouples growth from grants.

3. **Deep integrations with Sei protocols**  
   - As we integrate with Sei-native perps / DEXs / structured products, LiqPass becomes part of their core user experience.  
   - These integrations create recurring revenue-sharing arrangements and a strong incentive for us to maintain and improve the system long-term.

Operationally, we keep the stack lean (single focused product, compact infra) so that once initial development is funded, ongoing costs can be covered by premiums and partner revenue on Sei.

---

**中**

有。这个资助的目标，是帮助 LiqPass 在 Sei 上从“单机 MVP”走到“有真实用户和协议在用”，而不是长期依赖补贴生存。

资助之后，我们的可持续路径主要有三条：

1. **保费驱动的收入闭环**  
   - 用户为短期保障支付稳定币保费。  
   - 每一笔保费在链上拆分为三部分：（1）理赔资金池；（2）合作协议 / 生态伙伴分成；（3）LiqPass 自身的运营与研发。  
   - 在目标损失率约 60–70% 的前提下，剩余的风险加载与服务费可以覆盖基础设施与持续开发，不再依赖新增资助。

2. **链上承保与 LP 资金**  
   - 上线链上承保 / 资金池模块，让合作方和 LP 可以注入资金、按保费分成获得收益。  
   - 这样风险资金来自市场参与者，而不是依赖基金会持续输血，项目可以随着保费规模自然扩张。

3. **与 Sei 协议的深度集成**  
   - 随着与 Sei 原生永续合约、DEX、结构化产品等协议的集成，LiqPass 会成为这些应用用户体验的一部分。  
   - 这些集成带来持续的收益分成关系，也让我们有强烈动力长期维护和优化这套系统。

在执行层面，我们刻意保持产品和基础设施的**轻量与聚焦**，确保在完成这轮开发资助后，后续的运维与迭代成本，可以由 Sei 链上的保费收入和合作分成自然覆盖。

<!-- mid: b26d8476-7be7-45e0-aa4c-6e63fbb989a9 -->
**EN**

Two quick points:

1. **Built-first, then funded.**  
   LiqPass is not a slideware idea – there is already a working MVP on another chain (contracts + backend + web app + CEX verification). The goal of this grant is to **move that proven loop onto Sei**, specialize it for Sei’s ecosystem, and turn it into public-good risk infra that other teams can plug into.

2. **Open, verifiable, and reusable.**  
   We plan to keep the core logic and data as transparent as possible: on-chain evidence hashes, public dashboards, and (where reasonable) open-sourced components. This lets Sei teams reuse LiqPass as a **common risk layer** instead of each building their own opaque insurance fund, and it gives the community a clear view of how risk is priced and paid out on-chain.

If helpful, I am also happy to collaborate closely with Sei core devs and ecosystem teams to co-design the first integrations, and adjust the roadmap so LiqPass fills the most urgent “risk + user retention” gaps in the Sei trading stack.

---

**中**

补充两点关键信息：

1. **先做出来，再要钱。**  
   LiqPass 不是纸面概念，目前已经在另一条链上跑通了完整 MVP（合约 + 后端 + 前端 + CEX 验证闭环）。本次申请的目标，是把这条已经验证过的闭环**迁移并定制到 Sei**，让它变成 Sei 上可复用的风险基础设施，而不是从零起一个新故事。

2. **强调可验证与可复用。**  
   我们会尽量保持核心逻辑和数据的透明：证据哈希上链、公开看板，以及在合适范围内开放部分代码，让 Sei 上的其他项目可以把 LiqPass 当作一个**通用的风险缓冲层**来复用，而不是各自搞一个看不见里面在干什么的“保险池”。社区也能清楚看到风险是如何定价、如何赔付的。

如果基金会和 Sei 生态有优先级更高的集成方向，我也非常愿意跟核心开发者和协议项目一起协作，调整里程碑，让 LiqPass 优先补上 Sei 交易栈里最关键的“风险与留存”缺口。

### Project Idea *

EN:  
LiqPass is a liquidation protection layer for high-leverage traders. Users pay a small, fixed premium to cap their downside when trading perpetuals or margin products. If a covered position is liquidated within the insured window, LiqPass pays out according to transparent, pre-defined rules instead of subjective claim reviews.

The system is event-driven and verifiable:  
- On-chain contracts record policy purchases and payouts.  
- A backend service ingests read-only exchange data (e.g., liquidation events, PnL, leverage) and produces a minimal evidence bundle.  
- Claims are decided by deterministic rules, not human discretion.

On Movement Network, we plan to:  
1) Port the core “premium collection + payout” contracts and evidence-anchoring flow.  
2) Integrate with at least one perp venue or liquidity source in the Movement ecosystem.  
3) Ship a simple web UI where users connect wallets, buy coverage, and submit liquidation order IDs for verification.  

The goal is to give Movement traders a clear, budget-bounded way to use leverage more confidently, while driving volume and fee growth for on-chain venues.

ZH:  
LiqPass 是面向高杠杆交易者的“爆仓保护层”。用户支付一笔小而可控的保费，在做永续或杠杆交易时给自己设置一个亏损上限。如果在约定时间窗口内发生强平，LiqPass 会按事先公开的规则进行赔付，而不是走主观的人工理赔流程。

系统是“事件驱动 + 可验证”的：  
- 链上合约记录所有投保与赔付事件；  
- 后端只读取交易所数据（如强平事件、PnL、杠杆倍数），生成最小化的证据包；  
- 理赔完全由确定性的规则驱动，而非人工判断。

在 Movement Network 上，我们计划：  
1）迁移“保费收取 + 赔付”的核心合约和证据上链锚定流程；  
2）与至少一个基于 Movement 的合约交易场所或流动性来源集成；  
3）上线一个简洁的 Web 界面，让用户用钱包购买保障，并提交爆仓订单号进行验证。  

目标是在 Movement 上，为使用杠杆的交易者提供“有预算上限的下行保护”，同时为链上交易场景带来更高的交易量与手续费收入。

---

### Budget Proposal *

（你可以按需要把金额整体放大 / 缩小，比如 20k、30k、40k 美金等值 MOVE）

EN:  
**Total requested:** 30,000 USD equivalent in MOVE, tied to clear milestones.

- **10,000 USD** – Core protocol & testnet deployment  
  - Port and adapt LiqPass core contracts to Movement testnet.  
  - Implement the event-driven verification backend for one major CEX integration in test mode.  
  - Ship a basic web UI for policy purchase and claim submission on testnet.

- **10,000 USD** – Ecosystem integration & pilot UX  
  - Integrate with at least one Movement-based perp / DeFi venue (or a sandboxed demo market).  
  - Improve UX (wallet flows, bilingual UI, claim status pages) and add basic analytics (policies, premiums, claims).  
  - Run a small closed pilot with early users / partner community.

- **10,000 USD** – Mainnet beta & public pilot  
  - Deploy audited contracts to Movement mainnet (or mainnet beta, per schedule).  
  - Run a 3-month public pilot with guarded parameters (caps on coverage and payouts).  
  - Publish an open report (design, metrics, lessons and next steps).

ZH:  
**申请总额：** 30,000 美金等值 MOVE，全部与可验证里程碑挂钩。

- **10,000 美金** —— 协议与测试网部署  
  - 将 LiqPass 核心合约迁移并适配到 Movement 测试网；  
  - 实现针对一家主流 CEX 的事件验证后端（测试模式）；  
  - 上线测试网端到端 Demo：投保 + 提交订单号 + 返回验证结果。

- **10,000 美金** —— 生态集成与体验打磨  
  - 与至少一个基于 Movement 的永续 / DeFi 场景（或沙盒市场）集成；  
  - 优化用户体验（钱包流程、中英双语界面、理赔状态页），增加基础数据面板（保单数、保费、理赔数等）；  
  - 与早期社区 / 合作方开展小规模封闭试点。

- **10,000 美金** —— 主网 Beta 与公开试点  
  - 将审计后的合约部署到 Movement 主网（或主网 Beta）；  
  - 在严格风控参数下，运行 3 个月公开试点（覆盖额度和赔付额度设上限）；  
  - 输出一份公开报告（架构设计、试点数据、经验教训与下一步计划）。

---

### Current Project Metrics *

EN:  
LiqPass is currently a pre-launch MVP with the following progress:

- A minimal premium-collection contract is already deployed on an EVM L2 and used in small internal tests.  
- A backend service (TypeScript + Python) can read liquidation and order data from major CEXs via read-only API keys and normalize it into evidence JSON.  
- A working front-end demo exists with wallet connection, policy purchase flow, order-ID-based claim submission, and basic claim status views.  
- The system has been tested with dozens of simulated liquidation scenarios and a small group of early testers, but no public launch or paid marketing yet.

These components will be adapted and re-deployed natively on Movement Network as part of this grant.

ZH:  
目前 LiqPass 处于“预上线 MVP”阶段，进展如下：  

- 已在一个 EVM L2 上部署最小可用的保费收取合约，并完成小规模内部测试；  
- 后端服务（TypeScript + Python）已能通过只读 API 读取主流 CEX 的订单与强平数据，并规范化为证据 JSON；  
- 已有可用的前端 Demo，支持连接钱包、购买保障、按订单号提交理赔申请、查看基础理赔状态；  
- 已在内部完成数十个强平场景的模拟测试，并邀请少量早期用户试用，尚未公开上线或进行市场推广。  

上述能力会在本次资助中迁移并适配到 Movement Network 上。

---

### Team Background *

EN:  
LiqPass is led by a solo founder (zms / @CryptoNaxin) with a background in full-stack engineering and derivatives trading:

- 3+ years building and operating web apps using TypeScript, React, Node.js and Python.  
- Hands-on experience designing, deploying and verifying smart contracts on EVM L2s.  
- Direct trading experience with high-leverage perp products, which informs the product design, risk limits and UX.

The founder is already running the current MVP end-to-end (front-end, backend, contracts, and infra) and plans to bring in part-time collaborators for Move development and security review once the Movement integration phase begins.

ZH:  
LiqPass 目前由一名创始人（zms / @CryptoNaxin）主导，具备全栈开发与衍生品交易背景：  

- 3 年以上 TypeScript、React、Node.js、Python 等技术栈的 Web 应用开发与运维经验；  
- 具有在 EVM L2 上设计、部署、验证智能合约的实战经验；  
- 长期参与高杠杆合约交易，对爆仓风险、风控参数和用户行为有一线体感，这直接影响了产品的结构设计与 UX 取舍。  

当前 MVP 由创始人独立完成（前端、后端、合约与基础设施），在进入 Movement 集成阶段后，会逐步引入兼职协作者负责 Move 合约与安全审查。

---

### Quarterly Milestones *

（按“从获得资助开始的四个季度”来写）

EN:  

**Quarter 1 (Months 0–3): Movement testnet MVP**  
- Port core LiqPass contracts and deploy to Movement testnet.  
- Implement testnet evidence-anchoring (hashes / receipts) for at least one CEX integration in test mode.  
- Ship a basic bilingual (EN/ZH) front-end with end-to-end flow: connect wallet → buy coverage → submit liquidation order ID → view decision.  
- *Target KPIs:* 1 testnet deployment, ≥30 internal / testnet policies, ≥20 simulated liquidation claims.

**Quarter 2 (Months 3–6): Ecosystem integration & closed pilot**  
- Integrate with at least one Movement-based perp / DeFi venue or a sandboxed demo market.  
- Add basic analytics dashboard (policies, premium volume, claims, loss ratio).  
- Run a closed pilot with an early community or partner (e.g., 50–100 test users).  
- *Target KPIs:* ≥100 testnet policies sold, ≥50 claims processed (simulated or small-stakes), one ecosystem integration.

**Quarter 3 (Months 6–9): Mainnet beta launch**  
- Deploy audited contracts to Movement mainnet (or mainnet beta).  
- Enable small-size real policies with strict caps on coverage and daily risk budget.  
- Start collecting real-world usage data and feedback from early adopters.  
- *Target KPIs:* ≥100 mainnet policies, measurable premium volume (even if small), public docs and integration guide.

**Quarter 4 (Months 9–12): Scale-up & open tools**  
- Refine pricing parameters and risk limits based on pilot data.  
- Add at least one additional coverage SKU (e.g., 8-hour window or monthly drawdown cover).  
- Open-source the core verification + evidence modules so other Movement builders can reuse them.  
- Publish a public post-mortem and roadmap for scaling LiqPass on Movement.  
- *Target KPIs:* ≥300 cumulative mainnet policies, at least 1 external team reusing LiqPass components or integrating with the verification flow.

ZH:  

**第 1 季度（0–3 个月）：Movement 测试网 MVP**  
- 将 LiqPass 核心合约迁移并部署到 Movement 测试网；  
- 在测试网实现证据锚定（哈希 / 收据），完成至少一家 CEX 的测试模式集成；  
- 上线中英双语前端：连接钱包 → 购买保障 → 提交爆仓订单号 → 查看判定结果；  
- *目标指标：* 1 套测试网部署，≥30 个测试网保单，≥20 个模拟理赔场景。

**第 2 季度（3–6 个月）：生态集成与小规模试点**  
- 与至少一个基于 Movement 的永续 / DeFi 场景或沙盒市场完成集成；  
- 增加基础数据面板（保单数、保费规模、理赔数、赔付率等）；  
- 与早期社区或合作方开展封闭试点（约 50–100 名测试用户）；  
- *目标指标：* 测试网上累计 ≥100 份保单，≥50 笔理赔请求（可含小额真实或模拟），完成 1 个生态集成。

**第 3 季度（6–9 个月）：主网 Beta 上线**  
- 将审计后的合约部署到 Movement 主网（或主网 Beta）；  
- 在严格风控参数下开放小额真实投保（覆盖额度和每日风险预算受控）；  
- 开始采集真实用户行为数据与反馈，优化产品；  
- *目标指标：* 主网上累计 ≥100 份保单，形成可量化的保费体量（即便规模不大），发布公开文档与集成指南。

**第 4 季度（9–12 个月）：放量与开放工具**  
- 基于试点数据，迭代定价参数与风险限额；  
- 新增至少一个保障 SKU（例如 8 小时窗口保、月度回撤保等）；  
- 将核心“验证 + 证据模块”开源，方便 Movement 上其他项目复用；  
- 发布一份完整的试点复盘与后续扩展路线图；  
- *目标指标：* 主网上累计 ≥300 份保单，至少 1 个外部团队复用 LiqPass 组件或接入验证流程。

---

<!-- mid: 956aac5b-7288-48a2-a2a0-2bff5cb4d7f4 -->
Here’s a draft you can paste into the form:

---

LiqPass is a parametric “liquidation insurance” layer for high-leverage traders, powered by Chainlink oracles.

Today, retail traders who use 20–100x leverage on CEXs have almost no clean way to cap tail risk: either they over-hedge with complex options, or they blow up. Existing “insurance” products are opaque, manual, and hard to audit. LiqPass turns liquidation into a verifiable on-chain event and pays out according to transparent rules.

The core of the project is an open, Chainlink-powered “Liquidation Event Oracle” plus a reference dApp:

- Users buy short-term coverage (8h / 24h / monthly drawdown) for BTC/USDC or BTC/USDT positions.  
- If a position is liquidated or breaches a defined drawdown, a claim can be triggered and evaluated automatically.  
- Smart contracts handle policy lifecycle and capped payouts; all evidence is logged and reproducible.

Chainlink is how we make this robust and trust-minimized:

- **Chainlink Data Feeds** provide canonical reference prices for liquidation and drawdown checks, avoiding exchange-specific manipulation or wicks.  
- **Chainlink Functions** query CEX read-only APIs (or our verification endpoint) to fetch normalized order and liquidation data, then return a compact, hash-committed result on-chain.  
- **Chainlink Automation** manages coverage windows, claim deadlines, and scheduled risk resets so the system can run without manual cron jobs.

The oracle module and contracts will be fully open source and designed as reusable building blocks, so perps DEXs, structured-product protocols, and risk managers can plug into the same “liquidation oracle” standard.

Success looks like: a battle-tested Chainlink integration, at least one production integration with a derivatives protocol, and measurable usage (number of policies, total premium volume, claims processed) proving that Chainlink-secured liquidation data can safely back real parametric protection.

<!-- mid: b2f19937-782a-4717-b05a-7f9a96682e0f -->
**English version**

LiqPass is a parametric liquidation protection protocol for high-leverage traders, built as a Chainlink-powered risk layer any derivatives venue can plug into.

We will ship two main pieces:  
1) Smart contracts that sell short-term coverage (8h / 24h / monthly drawdown caps) for BTC/USDC and BTC/USDT leverage positions, manage policy lifecycle, and execute capped, rules-based payouts.  
2) A “liquidation evidence” oracle pipeline that turns CEX read-only data plus Chainlink infrastructure into a verifiable on-chain event.

When a user buys coverage, the policy terms (leverage, notional, coverage window, payout cap) are fixed on-chain. If the covered position is liquidated or breaches the agreed drawdown, a claim can be triggered and evaluated automatically.

Chainlink is how we make this robust:  
- **Chainlink Data Feeds** give canonical reference prices for liquidation and drawdown checks, independent of any single exchange.  
- **Chainlink Functions** call our verification endpoint and/or CEX read-only APIs, normalize order and liquidation data, and return a hash-committed result on-chain.  
- **Chainlink Automation** runs time-based logic: coverage windows, claim deadlines, cool-downs, and daily risk resets.

All LiqPass contracts and oracle modules will be open source, modular, and designed as reusable building blocks. Perps DEXs, options vaults, structured-product protocols, and risk desks can integrate the same liquidation oracle standard instead of rebuilding bespoke logic.

We expect to succeed by focusing on a narrow, high-pain segment (20–100x BTC perps), delivering a small but verifiable MVP on Base + Chainlink, and piloting with at least one derivatives venue. Success metrics: policies sold, premium volume, claims processed, and loss ratio over time, all backed by transparent, Chainlink-secured evidence.

---

**中文版（对应上面内容）**

LiqPass 是面向高杠杆交易者的参数化“爆仓保护”协议，是一层由 Chainlink 驱动的风险基础设施，任何衍生品平台都可以接入。

本项目将交付两大核心模块：  
1）一套智能合约，用于售卖短期保障（8 小时 / 24 小时 / 月度回撤上限），面向 BTC/USDC、BTC/USDT 的杠杆仓位，负责保单生命周期管理，并按预先约定的规则执行封顶赔付；  
2）一条“爆仓证据”预言机流水线，将中心化交易所只读数据结合 Chainlink 基础设施，转换为可在链上验证的事件。

用户购买保障后，保单条款（杠杆倍数、名义金额、保障时间窗、赔付上限）会固化在链上。当被保仓位发生爆仓或超过约定回撤阈值时，可以触发理赔，并由系统自动评估。

Chainlink 是我们实现稳健与可信的关键：  
- **Chainlink 价格预言机**提供标准化参考价格，用于判断是否爆仓或触及回撤，不依赖任何单一交易所；  
- **Chainlink Functions** 调用我们的验证端点和/或交易所只读 API，对订单与爆仓数据做归一化处理，并将结果以哈希承诺的形式写回链上；  
- **Chainlink Automation** 负责所有与时间相关的逻辑：保障时间窗、理赔截止时间、冷静期以及每日风险重置。

所有 LiqPass 合约与预言机模块将完全开源、模块化设计，作为可复用的积木：永续合约 DEX、期权金库、结构化产品协议以及风控团队，都可以直接集成统一的爆仓预言机标准，而无需各自重复造轮子。

我们的成功路径是：从一个窄而痛点极强的用户群体（20–100 倍 BTC 合约交易者）切入，在 Base + Chainlink 上先跑通一个小而可验证的 MVP，并与至少一家衍生品平台完成试点。核心衡量指标包括：售出的保单数量、累计保费规模、处理的理赔数量以及长期损失率，这些都将基于透明、由 Chainlink 保障的数据证据来统计。
